# DSM-5-TR Study Index

Auto-generated by `scripts/build_index.py` — do not hand-edit; rebuild after changing notes.

**How to use:** find a disorder below (A–Z, by code, or by feature), open its note, then check its *Differential diagnosis* and *Documentation cues* sections. Chapter overviews explain how neighbouring disorders are told apart. Page numbers are PDF pages of `references/source/DSM 5 TR-APA (2022).pdf`.

**Use limits:** these are study summaries of the 2022 edition, not independently clinically validated notes or a current billing-code source. Code lookup includes codes discussed in a note (including exclusions and associated conditions), so read its Coding section before use. Section III proposed conditions and the alternative personality model are labeled separately in index.json. Topic notes are included in the A–Z lookup. See [agent guidance](AGENT_GUIDE.md) and [progress](PROGRESS.md).

- [Chapters](#chapters)
- [Disorders A–Z](#disorders-az)
- [Lookup by ICD-10-CM code](#lookup-by-icd-10-cm-code)
- [Lookup by feature / symptom](#lookup-by-feature--symptom)
- [Aliases](#aliases)
- Reference tables: [alphabetical codes](reference/icd10-codes-alphabetical.md) · [numerical codes](reference/icd10-codes-numerical.md) · [classification navigation and source discrepancies](reference/classification-navigation.md)

- Original-source navigation: [classification](../source/dsm5tr_text/00-dsm-5-tr-classification.md) · [book subject index](../source/dsm5tr_text/supplement-book-index.md). Subject-index references use printed book pagination, not PDF page positions.

## Lookup by feature / symptom

Features as tagged in each note's front-matter. Use this to build a differential from presenting symptoms.

- **0-4 rating** — [Cross-Cutting Symptom Measures (Level 1 and Level 2)](chapters/26-assessment-measures/cross-cutting-symptom-measures.md)
- **0-4 scale** — [Severity Measures and Clinician-Rated Dimensions of Psychosis Symptom Severity](chapters/26-assessment-measures/severity-measures-and-psychosis-dimensions.md)
- **12 child domains** — [Cross-Cutting Symptom Measures (Level 1 and Level 2)](chapters/26-assessment-measures/cross-cutting-symptom-measures.md)
- **13 adult domains** — [Cross-Cutting Symptom Measures (Level 1 and Level 2)](chapters/26-assessment-measures/cross-cutting-symptom-measures.md)
- **20 disorder review groups** — [DSM History, DSM-5 Development, and DSM-5-TR Revision Process](chapters/01-section-i-basics/dsm-history-and-tr-revision.md)
- **200+ experts** — [DSM History, DSM-5 Development, and DSM-5-TR Revision Process](chapters/01-section-i-basics/dsm-history-and-tr-revision.md)
- **23 adult items** — [Cross-Cutting Symptom Measures (Level 1 and Level 2)](chapters/26-assessment-measures/cross-cutting-symptom-measures.md)
- **24-month research window** — [Suicidal Behavior Disorder](chapters/29-conditions-for-further-study/suicidal-behavior-disorder.md)
- **25 facets** — [Pathological Personality Trait Domains and Facets](chapters/28-alternative-dsm-5-model-for-personality-disorders/pathological-personality-traits.md)
- **25 parent items** — [Cross-Cutting Symptom Measures (Level 1 and Level 2)](chapters/26-assessment-measures/cross-cutting-symptom-measures.md)
- **2–3-day hypomanic periods** — [Depressive Episodes With Short-Duration Hypomania](chapters/29-conditions-for-further-study/depressive-episodes-with-short-duration-hypomania.md)
- **3-6 hz resting pill-rolling tremor** — [Medication-Induced Parkinsonism](chapters/24-medication-induced-movement-disorders-and-other-adverse-effe/medication-induced-parkinsonism.md)
- **30-day recall** — [WHODAS 2.0 Printed Form and Item Domains](chapters/26-assessment-measures/whodas-printed-form-and-item-domains.md)
- **36 items** — [WHODAS 2.0 Printed Form and Item Domains](chapters/26-assessment-measures/whodas-printed-form-and-item-domains.md), [World Health Organization Disability Assessment Schedule 2.0 (WHODAS 2.0)](chapters/26-assessment-measures/whodas-2.0.md)
- **4 cross-cutting review groups** — [DSM History, DSM-5 Development, and DSM-5-TR Revision Process](chapters/01-section-i-basics/dsm-history-and-tr-revision.md)
- **5 days in 1 year** — [Nonsuicidal Self-Injury Disorder](chapters/29-conditions-for-further-study/nonsuicidal-self-injury-disorder.md)
- **5 of 9 in 12 months** — [Internet Gaming Disorder](chapters/29-conditions-for-further-study/internet-gaming-disorder.md)
- **6 domains** — [World Health Organization Disability Assessment Schedule 2.0 (WHODAS 2.0)](chapters/26-assessment-measures/whodas-2.0.md)
- **6-month duration** — [Schizophrenia](chapters/05-schizophrenia-spectrum-and-other-psychotic-disorders/schizophrenia.md)
- **8 items** — [Severity Measures and Clinician-Rated Dimensions of Psychosis Symptom Severity](chapters/26-assessment-measures/severity-measures-and-psychosis-dimensions.md)
- **abnormal psychomotor behavior** — [Severity Measures and Clinician-Rated Dimensions of Psychosis Symptom Severity](chapters/26-assessment-measures/severity-measures-and-psychosis-dimensions.md)
- **aborted before initiation excluded** — [Suicidal Behavior and Nonsuicidal Self-Injury](chapters/25-other-conditions-that-may-be-a-focus-of-clinical-attention/suicidal-behavior-and-nonsuicidal-self-injury.md)
- **abrupt cessation or reduction** — [Caffeine Withdrawal](chapters/19-substance-related-and-addictive-disorders/caffeine-withdrawal.md)
- **abrupt surge of intense fear/discomfort** — [Panic Attack Specifier](chapters/08-anxiety-disorders/panic-attack-specifier.md)
- **absent at rest** — [Medication-Induced Postural Tremor](chapters/24-medication-induced-movement-disorders-and-other-adverse-effe/medication-induced-postural-tremor.md)
- **absent in sleep** — [Tardive Dyskinesia](chapters/24-medication-induced-movement-disorders-and-other-adverse-effe/tardive-dyskinesia.md)
- **absent respiratory effort** — [Central Sleep Apnea](chapters/15-sleep-wake-disorders/central-sleep-apnea.md)
- **acculturation** — [Problems Related to the Social Environment](chapters/25-other-conditions-that-may-be-a-focus-of-clinical-attention/social-environment-problems.md)
- **achievement** — [Educational Problems](chapters/25-other-conditions-that-may-be-a-focus-of-clinical-attention/educational-problems.md)
- **across modalities (spoken/written/sign)** — [Language Disorder](chapters/04-neurodevelopmental-disorders/language-disorder.md)
- **acted on urges or marked distress/interpersonal difficulty** — [Pedophilic Disorder](chapters/22-paraphilic-disorders/pedophilic-disorder.md)
- **acted on with nonconsenting person or distress/impairment** — [Exhibitionistic Disorder](chapters/22-paraphilic-disorders/exhibitionistic-disorder.md), [Frotteuristic Disorder](chapters/22-paraphilic-disorders/frotteuristic-disorder.md), [Voyeuristic Disorder](chapters/22-paraphilic-disorders/voyeuristic-disorder.md)
- **active avoidance or endurance with intense fear** — [Specific Phobia](chapters/08-anxiety-disorders/specific-phobia.md)
- **active versus recovery care** — [Child Maltreatment and Neglect Problems](chapters/25-other-conditions-that-may-be-a-focus-of-clinical-attention/child-maltreatment-and-neglect.md)
- **activities given up** — [Alcohol Use Disorder](chapters/19-substance-related-and-addictive-disorders/alcohol-use-disorder.md)
- **activity interference** — [Stereotypic Movement Disorder](chapters/04-neurodevelopmental-disorders/stereotypic-movement-disorder.md)
- **acute anxiety** — [Antidepressant Discontinuation Syndrome](chapters/24-medication-induced-movement-disorders-and-other-adverse-effe/antidepressant-discontinuation-syndrome.md)
- **acute dissociative reactions under 1 month** — [Other Specified and Unspecified Dissociative Disorder](chapters/11-dissociative-disorders/other-specified-and-unspecified.md)
- **acute onset hours to days** — [Delirium](chapters/20-neurocognitive-disorders/delirium.md)
- **acute substance-linked syndrome** — [Cannabis Intoxication](chapters/19-substance-related-and-addictive-disorders/cannabis-intoxication.md), [Inhalant Intoxication](chapters/19-substance-related-and-addictive-disorders/inhalant-intoxication.md), [Other (or Unknown) Substance Intoxication](chapters/19-substance-related-and-addictive-disorders/other-or-unknown-substance-intoxication.md), [Other (or Unknown) Substance Withdrawal](chapters/19-substance-related-and-addictive-disorders/other-or-unknown-substance-withdrawal.md), [Other Hallucinogen Intoxication](chapters/19-substance-related-and-addictive-disorders/other-hallucinogen-intoxication.md), [Phencyclidine Intoxication](chapters/19-substance-related-and-addictive-disorders/phencyclidine-intoxication.md)
- **adaptive deficits in conceptual/social/practical domains** — [Intellectual Developmental Disorder (Intellectual Disability)](chapters/04-neurodevelopmental-disorders/intellectual-developmental-disorder.md)
- **adaptive functioning** — [Neurobehavioral Disorder Associated With Prenatal Alcohol Exposure](chapters/29-conditions-for-further-study/neurobehavioral-disorder-associated-with-prenatal-alcohol-exposure.md)
- **additional code not a disorder** — [No Diagnosis or Condition](chapters/23-other-mental-disorders-and-additional-codes/no-diagnosis-or-condition.md)
- **additional cognitive disturbance** — [Delirium](chapters/20-neurocognitive-disorders/delirium.md)
- **adequate opportunity for sleep** — [Insomnia Disorder](chapters/15-sleep-wake-disorders/insomnia-disorder.md)
- **adjustment difficulty** — [Phase of Life Problem](chapters/25-other-conditions-that-may-be-a-focus-of-clinical-attention/phase-of-life-problem.md)
- **adjustment exclusions** — [Other Specified and Unspecified Anxiety Disorder](chapters/08-anxiety-disorders/other-specified-and-unspecified.md)
- **adjustment-like disorder with delayed onset** — [Other Specified and Unspecified Trauma- and Stressor-Related Disorder](chapters/10-trauma-and-stressor-related-disorders/other-specified-and-unspecified.md)
- **adjustment-like disorder with prolonged duration** — [Other Specified and Unspecified Trauma- and Stressor-Related Disorder](chapters/10-trauma-and-stressor-related-disorders/other-specified-and-unspecified.md)
- **adults 18+** — [World Health Organization Disability Assessment Schedule 2.0 (WHODAS 2.0)](chapters/26-assessment-measures/whodas-2.0.md)
- **affective instability from mood reactivity** — [Borderline Personality Disorder](chapters/21-personality-disorders/borderline-personality-disorder.md)
- **affective lability** — [Personality Change Due to Another Medical Condition](chapters/21-personality-disorders/personality-change-due-to-another-medical-condition.md), [Premenstrual Dysphoric Disorder](chapters/07-depressive-disorders/premenstrual-dysphoric-disorder.md)
- **age ≥18** — [Antisocial Personality Disorder](chapters/21-personality-disorders/antisocial-personality-disorder.md), [Voyeuristic Disorder](chapters/22-paraphilic-disorders/voyeuristic-disorder.md)
- **age ≥4 (or developmental equivalent)** — [Encopresis](chapters/14-elimination-disorders/encopresis.md)
- **age ≥5 (or developmental equivalent)** — [Enuresis](chapters/14-elimination-disorders/enuresis.md)
- **agent capable of causing it** — [Substance/Medication-Induced Sexual Dysfunction](chapters/16-sexual-dysfunctions/substance-medication-induced-sexual-dysfunction.md)
- **aggression** — [Personality Change Due to Another Medical Condition](chapters/21-personality-disorders/personality-change-due-to-another-medical-condition.md), [Stimulant Use Disorder](chapters/19-substance-related-and-addictive-disorders/stimulant-use-disorder.md)
- **agitation not influenced by external stimuli** — [Catatonia Associated With Another Mental Disorder (Catatonia Specifier)](chapters/05-schizophrenia-spectrum-and-other-psychotic-disorders/catatonia-associated-with-another-mental-disorder.md)
- **alcohol on breath** — [Alcohol Intoxication](chapters/19-substance-related-and-addictive-disorders/alcohol-intoxication.md)
- **alcohol-related symptoms causing distress or impairment** — [Unspecified Alcohol-Related Disorder](chapters/19-substance-related-and-addictive-disorders/unspecified-alcohol-related-disorder.md)
- **alert after awakening** — [Rapid Eye Movement Sleep Behavior Disorder](chapters/15-sleep-wake-disorders/rem-sleep-behavior-disorder.md)
- **altered motor or sensory function** — [Functional Neurological Symptom Disorder (Conversion Disorder)](chapters/12-somatic-symptom-and-related-disorders/functional-neurological-symptom-disorder.md)
- **altered sense of reality** — [Acute Stress Disorder](chapters/10-trauma-and-stressor-related-disorders/acute-stress-disorder.md)
- **altered sense of time** — [Depersonalization/Derealization Disorder](chapters/11-dissociative-disorders/depersonalization-derealization-disorder.md)
- **alternative names in parentheses** — [Coding and Recording Procedures](chapters/01-section-i-basics/coding-and-recording-procedures.md)

- **with mood-congruent psychotic features** — [Specifiers for Depressive Disorders](chapters/07-depressive-disorders/specifiers.md)
- **with mood-incongruent psychotic features** — [Specifiers for Depressive Disorders](chapters/07-depressive-disorders/specifiers.md)
- **with peripartum onset** — [Cultural, Social Structural, and Sex/Gender Considerations](chapters/01-section-i-basics/cultural-considerations.md), [Specifiers for Bipolar and Related Disorders](chapters/06-bipolar-and-related-disorders/specifiers.md), [Specifiers for Depressive Disorders](chapters/07-depressive-disorders/specifiers.md)
- **with psychotic features (mood-congruent/incongruent)** — [Specifiers for Bipolar and Related Disorders](chapters/06-bipolar-and-related-disorders/specifiers.md)
- **with rapid cycling** — [Specifiers for Bipolar and Related Disorders](chapters/06-bipolar-and-related-disorders/specifiers.md)
- **with seasonal pattern** — [Specifiers for Bipolar and Related Disorders](chapters/06-bipolar-and-related-disorders/specifiers.md), [Specifiers for Depressive Disorders](chapters/07-depressive-disorders/specifiers.md)
- **withdrawal** — [Caffeine Use Disorder](chapters/29-conditions-for-further-study/caffeine-use-disorder.md), [Opioid Use Disorder](chapters/19-substance-related-and-addictive-disorders/opioid-use-disorder.md), [Sedative, Hypnotic, or Anxiolytic Use Disorder](chapters/19-substance-related-and-addictive-disorders/sedative-hypnotic-anxiolytic-use-disorder.md)
- **withdrawal delirium** — [Sedative, Hypnotic, or Anxiolytic Withdrawal](chapters/19-substance-related-and-addictive-disorders/sedative-hypnotic-anxiolytic-withdrawal.md)
- **withdrawal or medication exposure** — [Substance/Medication-Induced Sexual Dysfunction](chapters/16-sexual-dysfunctions/substance-medication-induced-sexual-dysfunction.md)
- **withdrawal or relief drinking** — [Alcohol Use Disorder](chapters/19-substance-related-and-addictive-disorders/alcohol-use-disorder.md)
- **within approximately one week** — [Cannabis Withdrawal](chapters/19-substance-related-and-addictive-disorders/cannabis-withdrawal.md)
- **witnessed breathing pauses** — [Obstructive Sleep Apnea Hypopnea](chapters/15-sleep-wake-disorders/obstructive-sleep-apnea-hypopnea.md)
- **word-finding problems** — [Language Disorder](chapters/04-neurodevelopmental-disorders/language-disorder.md)
- **workplace harassment** — [Occupational Problems](chapters/25-other-conditions-that-may-be-a-focus-of-clinical-attention/occupational-problems.md)
- **worry about harm to attachment figures** — [Separation Anxiety Disorder](chapters/08-anxiety-disorders/separation-anxiety-disorder.md)
- **worry about untoward event causing separation** — [Separation Anxiety Disorder](chapters/08-anxiety-disorders/separation-anxiety-disorder.md)
- **worse at rest** — [Restless Legs Syndrome](chapters/15-sleep-wake-disorders/restless-legs-syndrome.md)
- **worst point** — [Severity Measures and Clinician-Rated Dimensions of Psychosis Symptom Severity](chapters/26-assessment-measures/severity-measures-and-psychosis-dimensions.md)
- **worthlessness/guilt** — [Major Depressive Disorder](chapters/07-depressive-disorders/major-depressive-disorder.md)
- **yawning** — [Opioid Withdrawal](chapters/19-substance-related-and-addictive-disorders/opioid-withdrawal.md)
- **z codes four reasons to list** — [Terminology Notes and Section II Conventions](chapters/01-section-i-basics/terminology-and-section-ii-conventions.md)
- **~75-100% of occasions** — [Delayed Ejaculation](chapters/16-sexual-dysfunctions/delayed-ejaculation.md), [Erectile Disorder](chapters/16-sexual-dysfunctions/erectile-disorder.md), [Female Orgasmic Disorder](chapters/16-sexual-dysfunctions/female-orgasmic-disorder.md), [Premature (Early) Ejaculation](chapters/16-sexual-dysfunctions/premature-early-ejaculation.md)
- **≥1 hypomanic episode** — [Bipolar II Disorder](chapters/06-bipolar-and-related-disorders/bipolar-ii-disorder.md)
- **≥1 major depressive episode** — [Bipolar II Disorder](chapters/06-bipolar-and-related-disorders/bipolar-ii-disorder.md)
- **≥1 observed movement (fidgeting/leg swinging, rocking foot to foot, pacing, can't sit/stand still)** — [Medication-Induced Acute Akathisia](chapters/24-medication-induced-movement-disorders-and-other-adverse-effe/medication-induced-acute-akathisia.md)
- **≥12 months** — [Disruptive Mood Dysregulation Disorder](chapters/07-depressive-disorders/disruptive-mood-dysregulation-disorder.md)
- **≥1×/month for 3 months** — [Encopresis](chapters/14-elimination-disorders/encopresis.md)
- **≥2 of cognition/affectivity/interpersonal/impulse control** — [General Personality Disorder](chapters/21-personality-disorders/general-personality-disorder.md)
- **≥2 settings** — [Disruptive Mood Dysregulation Disorder](chapters/07-depressive-disorders/disruptive-mood-dysregulation-disorder.md)
- **≥2 weeks delusions or hallucinations without mood episode** — [Schizoaffective Disorder](chapters/05-schizophrenia-spectrum-and-other-psychotic-disorders/schizoaffective-disorder.md)
- **≥2 years (≥1 year youth)** — [Persistent Depressive Disorder](chapters/07-depressive-disorders/persistent-depressive-disorder.md)
- **≥2×/week for 3 months or clinically significant distress/impairment** — [Enuresis](chapters/14-elimination-disorders/enuresis.md)
- **≥3 months drba exposure (≥1 month if ≥60 y)** — [Tardive Dyskinesia](chapters/24-medication-induced-movement-disorders-and-other-adverse-effe/tardive-dyskinesia.md)
- **≥3 of 12** — [Catatonia Associated With Another Mental Disorder (Catatonia Specifier)](chapters/05-schizophrenia-spectrum-and-other-psychotic-disorders/catatonia-associated-with-another-mental-disorder.md)
- **≥3 of 12 catatonic features** — [Catatonic Disorder Due to Another Medical Condition](chapters/05-schizophrenia-spectrum-and-other-psychotic-disorders/catatonic-disorder-due-to-another-medical-condition.md)
- **≥4 of 13 symptoms** — [Panic Attack Specifier](chapters/08-anxiety-disorders/panic-attack-specifier.md)
- **≥4 of 13 symptoms peaking within minutes** — [Panic Disorder](chapters/08-anxiety-disorders/panic-disorder.md)
- **≥5 central apneas per hour on polysomnography** — [Central Sleep Apnea](chapters/15-sleep-wake-disorders/central-sleep-apnea.md)

- opioid-related disorder NOS → [Unspecified Opioid-Related Disorder](chapters/19-substance-related-and-addictive-disorders/unspecified-opioid-related-disorder.md)
- sedative-related disorder NOS, benzodiazepine-related disorder unspecified → [Unspecified Sedative-, Hypnotic-, or Anxiolytic-Related Disorder](chapters/19-substance-related-and-addictive-disorders/unspecified-sedative-hypnotic-anxiolytic-related-disorder.md)
- unspecified cocaine-related disorder, unspecified amphetamine-type substance-related disorder, stimulant-related disorder NOS → [Unspecified Stimulant-Related Disorder](chapters/19-substance-related-and-addictive-disorders/unspecified-stimulant-related-disorder.md)
- clinical judgment, cookbook use, criteria as guidelines, near-threshold diagnosis → [Use of Clinical Judgment](chapters/01-section-i-basics/use-of-clinical-judgment.md)
- voyeurism (when disordered), peeping → [Voyeuristic Disorder](chapters/22-paraphilic-disorders/voyeuristic-disorder.md)
- WHODAS printed form, WHODAS item map, WHODAS school work skip instruction → [WHODAS 2.0 Printed Form and Item Domains](chapters/26-assessment-measures/whodas-printed-form-and-item-domains.md)
- WHODAS, WHODAS 2.0, WHO disability schedule, disability assessment → [World Health Organization Disability Assessment Schedule 2.0 (WHODAS 2.0)](chapters/26-assessment-measures/whodas-2.0.md)
